Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the probability space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 864 and the convergence rate is O(281/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the probability space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 864 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 281. QED.
